## Open defect-ledger dispositions

Current read-only `def-alternating-k-linear-map` raw hash `6b881d2bb3c8fca6c1af5d1b4a348100cb32dfefc31fe27fb95e3958fb66f4d8`. Its Remarks still call the characteristic-two swapped-argument condition a tautology. Over F2, the bilinear map `f(v,w)=v1*w2` on F2^2 has `f(e1,e2)=1`, `f(e2,e1)=0`: skew symmetry becomes substantive symmetry. The repeated-argument definition remains correct. Conrad's Definition2.1, Theorem2.8 and converse qualification2.10 were checked. Original mathematics maintenance remains required; no imported edit or new consumer-context seal is asserted.
- `physics-em-sr-40-2026-10-08-5a-batch-24-stokes-curl-regularity` to its `-step8-deferred` successor. Current read-only Stokes supplier raw hash `fb6e1f112f643620ac5277f6c6dfacd0627aebd77bc921312e29e8ec411d9c5a`. Proof5.1 still claims a C1 curl for a C1 field. For `F=(0,0,x1*abs(x1))`, `curl F=(0,-2*abs(x1),0)` is continuous and fails differentiability at zero. The flux definition only needs continuity. This surplus regularity error does not refute the conditional patch identity. Original mathematics maintenance remains required.
